TL;DR: 提出了一种基于强化学习的框架，用于生成最优液体燃料反应器，以改善燃气轮机燃烧室中的稀薄吹熄（LBO）预测。该框架采用多阶段聚类-分类策略，通过强化学习代理合并微簇形成最优反应器区域，相比传统方法和k-means聚类提高了预测精度，同时大幅降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的聚类边界确定方法依赖人工启发式或输入空间中的距离度量，无法直接针对目标指标（如LBO预测精度）进行优化，导致反应器网络模型预测性能受限。

Method: 采用多阶段聚类-分类策略：首先通过初始聚类（如k-means）生成大量同质微簇，然后使用演员-评论家强化学习代理将这些微簇合并为最优反应器区域，显式地将目标指标（LBO预测精度）纳入聚类过程。

Result: 使用Jet-A机理（119种物种、841个反应）的验证表明，RL框架相比k-means聚类方法具有更好的预测精度，能够正确捕获LBO趋势，同时相比高保真计算模型实现了显著的计算加速。

Conclusion: 该RL驱动的方法作为一种计算高效的降阶建模技术，具有很大潜力，能够补充高保真仿真，用于快速设计空间探索。

Abstract: This study introduces a reinforcement learning (RL) framework for generating optimal liquid-fueled reactors to improve lean blowout (LBO) predictions in gas turbine combustors. Existing approaches for determining cluster boundaries rely on manual heuristics or distance-based metrics in the input space. In contrast, the proposed method is goal-oriented, explicitly accounting for the target metric (e.g., LBO prediction accuracy) during cluster formation. The framework employs a multi-stage clustering--classification strategy: an initial clustering step (e.g., $k$-means clustering) generates a large set of homogeneous micro-clusters, followed by an actor-critic RL agent that merges them into optimal reactor zones. The validation study, performed using a Jet-A mechanism (119 species, 841 reactions), shows the RL framework offers improved predictive fidelity compared to $k$-means and captures the correct LBO trends, while achieving substantial speedups relative to the high-fidelity computational model. Overall, the RL-driven approach demonstrates strong potential as a computationally efficient reduced-order modeling technique that can complement high-fidelity simulations for rapid design-space exploration.

</details>


### [192] [Real-time optimal control with shallow recurrent decoder networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19302)
*Matteo Tomasetto,Francesco Braghin,J. Nathan Kutz,Andrea Manzoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用浅层循环解码器网络降阶建模（SHRED-ROM）合成高维参数化动力系统的实时闭环控制器，仅依赖有限传感器读数，通过模仿专家示范在多个场景下实现高效分布式控制，并在潜空间层面引入传感器预测器以应对传感器故障或延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最优控制问题在处理高维时空动力学时，需要多次系统仿真，计算负担沉重，难以在多变场景下实现实时自适应控制。

Method: 基于SHRED-ROM构建降阶模型，利用少量传感器数据在潜空间进行实时控制；通过专家示范数据训练模型以模仿最优行为；同时合成潜层级的传感器预测器用于闭环反馈，缓解传感器故障或延迟问题。

Result: 在参数化密度控制和流体流动控制三个高维挑战性案例上验证了所提策略的有效性。

Conclusion: SHRED-ROM能够有效利用有限传感器数据实现高维参数化动力系统的实时最优控制，克服维数灾难，且闭环预测机制增强了系统对传感器异常的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Controlling dynamical systems in real-time across multiple scenarios is critical to enabling adaptive control strategies, ensuring stability and efficiency. However, to tailor control actions in response to varying scenarios, traditional optimal control problems typically require several system simulations, which are often computationally demanding due to the high-dimensionality of the underlying spatio-temporal dynamics. In this work, we exploit SHallow REcurrent Decoder networks-based Reduced Order Modeling (SHRED-ROM) to synthesize a real-time closed-loop controller for high-dimensional and parametric dynamics, relying solely on limited state sensor readings. After training the model on a few optimal examples given by an expert demonstrator, SHRED-ROM mimics the expert behavior with effective distributed control actions in new scenarios, alleviating the curse of dimensionality. Moreover, a sensor forecaster is synthesized and used to close the loop at the latent level, thus efficiently mitigating possible sensor failures or delays. The performance of the proposed optimal control strategy is finally assessed on three challenging high-dimensional cases dealing with either parametric density control or fluid flow control.

</details>


### [193] [Riemannian Deep Learning:Modules, Networks, and Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19305)
*Chen Ziheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一个统一的黎曼深度学习框架，从可复用神经模块、流形特定网络架构和底层几何设计三个角度，将批归一化、多项逻辑回归等核心组件推广到李群、陀螺群、SPD流形等一般黎曼流形，并设计了双曲空间、相关矩阵等几何表示的新型网络以及可学习的Log-Euclidean和Cholesky度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流形值表示的深度神经网络方法存在三个主要局限：许多基本组件局限于特定流形、依赖欧几里得近似、或需要计算昂贵且数值不稳定的几何操作。缺乏一个统一的黎曼深度学习框架。

Method: 从三个互补视角构建统一框架：1) 可复用神经模块：将批归一化从欧氏空间推广到李群和陀螺群，将多项逻辑回归推广到SPD流形及一般黎曼流形；2) 流形特定网络架构：提出双曲空间的无约束模型、Busemann基双曲学习、满秩相关矩阵网络；3) 底层几何设计：在SPD流形上引入可学习的Log-Euclidean几何和快速稳定的Cholesky基几何。

Result: 提出方法通过理论分析验证，并在视觉、信号处理、图学习和基因组学等应用中通过数值实验证明了有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为黎曼深度学习提供了统一的数学框架，解决了现有方法的泛化性、数值稳定性和计算效率问题，具有广泛的应用价值。

Abstract: Deep neural networks on manifold-valued representations have attracted growing interest, but many basic components remain tied to specific manifolds, rely on Euclidean approximations, or require costly and numerically fragile geometric operations. This thesis develops a unified framework for Riemannian deep learning from three complementary perspectives: reusable neural modules, manifold-specific network architectures, and the design of underlying geometries. It generalizes batch normalization from Euclidean spaces and individual manifolds to broad classes of Lie groups and gyrogroups, and extends multinomial logistic regression from Euclidean space to SPD manifolds and then to general Riemannian manifolds. It further develops neural networks for several important geometric representations, including an unconstrained model of hyperbolic space, Busemann-based hyperbolic learning, and full-rank correlation matrices. Finally, it introduces adaptive and computationally efficient Riemannian metrics on SPD manifolds, including learnable Log-Euclidean geometries and fast, stable Cholesky-based geometries. The proposed methods are supported by theoretical analysis and validated through numerical experiments and applications in vision, signal processing, graph learning, and genomics.

</details>


### [194] [Staypoint Detection from Noisy Trajectory Data [Experiment Paper]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19312)
*Lance Kennedy,Hossein Amiri,Yueyang Liu,Riyang Bao,Hanqi Chen,Mohammad Hashemi,Ruochen Kong,Xiaotong Liu,Joon-Seok Kim,Shengpu Tang,Liang Zhao,Andreas Züfle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对轨迹数据中停留点检测缺乏标准基准和系统评估的问题，贡献了16个大规模仿真数据集（含真实标注）和9种检测算法的评估，发现现有SOTA算法在真实噪声下表现不佳，而提出的无监督方法有显著改进，监督方法则大幅超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 停留点检测是语义轨迹分析的基础，但缺乏标准基准和公开的带标注数据集，导致现有算法从未被系统评估过。

Method: 构建16个大规模仿真数据集（含数千智能体及真实停留点标注，覆盖不同轨迹噪声水平），系统评估9种停留点检测算法（含SOTA和新方法），分析其对噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 现有SOTA算法在真实噪声条件下表现不佳；提出的无监督方法带来实质性提升；监督方法大幅超越现有基线。

Conclusion: 所提供的数据集和方法仅作为未来停留点检测研究的起点，该领域仍有较大改进空间。

Abstract: Detecting staypoints from raw trajectory data is fundamental to numerous spatial computing applications. This process transforms raw numeric sequences of geolocations into semantically meaningful locations, such as homes, workplaces, or restaurants. Despite its importance for semantic trajectory analysis, staypoint detection lacks standard benchmarks, and existing algorithms have never been systematically evaluated. This gap persists because no publicly available datasets provide both raw individual trajectories and ground-truth staypoint annotations. This benchmark paper addresses this limitation with two key contributions: (1) we introduce 16 large-scale simulated datasets capturing thousands of agents with annotated staypoints across varying trajectory noise levels, and (2) we evaluate nine staypoint detection algorithms-including both state-of-the-art and novel methods-to analyze their robustness to noise. Our evaluation reveals that existing state-of-the-art algorithms perform poorly under realistic noise conditions. Conversely, our proposed unsupervised methods yield substantial improvements, while supervised approaches drastically outperform existing baselines. While these results are very promising, these datasets and methods are only meant as starting points for future research in staypoint detection.

</details>


### [195] [Off-Context GRPO: Learning to Reason on Hard Problems using Privileged Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19313)
*Priyank Agrawal,Ankur Samanta,Shervin Ghasemlou,Jalaj Bhandari,Kavosh Asadi,Daniel Jiang,Aditya Modi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Off-Context GRPO (OC-GRPO)，通过在训练中提供特权引导（如解题前缀）生成off-context rollout，并引入重要性校正目标函数，解决了标准RLVR在难题上因零奖励导致的学习悬崖问题，在数学推理基准上平均提升3.9%（相对13.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLVR方法在难题上失效：当模型无法生成任何正确解时，奖励信号为零，模型陷入学习悬崖，无法获得有效梯度更新。

Method: 提出OC-GRPO，对GRPO做最小修改：(1) 在训练提示中加入特权引导（如部分解题步骤）生成off-context rollout；(2) 引入重要性校正目标函数，将更新方向校正回原始无引导目标，避免引导训练中的分布偏移导致训练不稳定。

Result: 在标准数学推理基准上，OC-GRPO相比原始GRPO平均获得3.9%的绝对提升（13.8%的相对提升），且额外计算成本可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 提供特权引导并配合重要性校正是一种轻量且有效的策略，能够克服RLVR在困难推理任务中的学习信号缺失问题，显著提升推理能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves reasoning in large language models. Yet, typical RLVR approaches fail on difficult problems: when a model cannot generate any correct solutions, it receives \textit{zero} learning signal. Providing privileged guidance during training, such as solution prefixes, can help overcome this learning cliff by steering the model towards {correct solutions with non-zero reward}. {We call these rollouts \textit{off-context}: they are generated from a training prompt that contains privileged guidance, while the target objective is defined by the original prompt without that guidance.} {We introduce} Off-Context GRPO (OC-GRPO), a minimally modified variant of GRPO that uses guided rollouts but applies an importance-corrected objective to steer the update back toward the original unguided objective, avoiding the mismatch that destabilizes uncorrected guided training. Empirically, our algorithm achieves a 3.9\% absolute improvement (13.8\% relative gain) over vanilla GRPO on average across standard mathematical reasoning benchmarks with negligible additional cost.

</details>


### [196] [CircuitKIT : Circuit Discovery, Evaluation, and Application Toolkit for Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19317)
*Pratinav Seth,Hem Gosalia,Aditya Kasliwal,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CircuitKIT是一个开源的电路分析库，通过类型化、可序列化的表示连接电路分析工作流，集成了发现算法、声明式接口、诊断工具和下游应用模块，为电路分析的执行与比较提供了统一基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前电路分析流程碎片化严重，发现、评估和干预等步骤需拼接不同实现，且对比性提示需手动编写，导致方法难以比较且应用受限。

Method: CircuitKIT通过类型化、可序列化的表示连接整个电路分析工作流，提供一套发现算法、声明式接口（用于将结构化数据映射为发现任务）、互补的电路诊断工具以及下游应用模块。

Result: 提供了一个统一的基础设施，支持电路分析的执行与比较，并附带代码、示例、笔记本和文档。

Conclusion: CircuitKIT有效解决了电路分析流程的碎片化问题，为模型解释和下游干预（剪枝、编辑、引导、选择性微调）提供了统一、可复现的平台。

Abstract: Circuit analysis can support not only model explanation but also downstream interventions such as pruning, editing, steering, and selective fine-tuning. However, conducting such analyses currently requires stitching together separate implementations for discovery, evaluation, and intervention, as well as hand-authoring the contrastive prompts required by many discovery methods. This fragmentation makes methods difficult to compare and limits their application beyond canonical tasks. We introduce CircuitKIT, a source-available library that connects the circuit-analysis workflow through a typed, serializable representation. CircuitKIT provides a suite of discovery algorithms, declarative interfaces for mapping structured data into discovery tasks, complementary circuit diagnostics, and downstream application modules. Together, these components provide common infrastructure for conducting and comparing circuit analyses. The library, examples, notebooks, and documentation are released at https://github.com/Lexsi-Labs/CircuitKIT .

</details>


### [197] [ISO: An RLVR-Native Optimization Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19331)
*Hanqing Zhu,Wenyan Cong,Zhizhou Sha,Sagnik Mukherjee,Xinyuan Song,David González-Martínez,Xiaoxia Wu,Yuandong Tian,Shiwei Liu,David Z. Pan,Zhangyang "Atlas" Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出等谱优化（ISO）框架，揭示了RLVR训练中权重谱的继承机制：基模型的权重谱保持不变，仅优化输入/输出奇异帧（奇异向量）。ISO包括离线合并（ISO-Merger）和在线优化（ISO-Optimizer）两种实现，在推理和编程任务上提升了训练效率与模型聚合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽能提升语言模型的推理能力，但其将奖励反馈转化为权重更新的优化层机制尚不明确。现有方法直接沿用预训练优化策略，忽略了后训练阶段奖励驱动适应的结构特性，作者希望揭示并利用这一缺失的优化层。

Method: （1）通过模型权重的奇异结构分析，发现RLVR训练会复用基模型的权重谱（奇异值），仅改变输入/输出奇异帧（奇异向量）。（2）基于此提出等谱优化（ISO）框架：离线ISO-Merger通过合并共享基模型的帧变化实现无数据融合；在线ISO-Optimizer固定基模型谱，仅用AdamW/Muon等优化器更新帧变量。

Result: ISO-Merger在无数据融合方法中取得最强聚合性能。ISO-Optimizer在1.5B~8B参数的推理/编程任务上，以更少训练步骤达到匹配准确率：Qwen3-8B-Base上，AdamW需270步达0.495，ISO-AdamW仅100步达相同准确率，210步进一步提升至0.509。

Conclusion: ISO为RLVR提供了明确的优化层答案：后训练不应全盘继承预训练优化策略，而应围绕奖励驱动适应的结构来设计——继承谱，优化帧。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is rapidly advancing the reasoning capabilities of language models, yet the optimization layer that converts reward feedback into weight-space updates remains poorly understood. Building on our prior analysis (Zhu et al., 2025), we study this missing layer through the singular structure of model weights and identify spectral inheritance: RLVR can reuse the base model's weight spectra while acquiring new behavior through changes in the associated input and output singular frames.
  We operationalize spectral inheritance as Isospectral Optimization (ISO), an RLVR-native, fixed-spectrum optimization framework with complementary offline and online instantiations. Offline, ISO-Merger combines the frame changes of shared-base specialists into a single fixed-spectrum model, requiring no post-merge data, rollouts, gradient updates, or on-policy distillation (OPD). It recovers complementary specialist capabilities and achieves the strongest aggregate performance among the compared data-free merging methods. Online, ISO-Optimizer applies a chosen base optimizer, including AdamW and Muon, to the frame variables while keeping the base spectra fixed. Across reasoning and coding tasks ranging from 1.5B to 8B parameters, ISO-Optimizer improves accuracy in the reported runs and reaches matched scores with substantially fewer training steps. On Qwen3-8B-Base, AdamW reaches an aggregate accuracy of 0.495 after 270 training steps. ISO-AdamW reaches the same accuracy after only 100 training steps and improves further to 0.509 after 210 training steps. Together, ISO offers a concrete answer to RLVR's missing optimization layer: rather than inheriting pre-training optimization wholesale, design post-training around the structure of reward-driven adaptation: inherit the spectrum, optimize the frames.

</details>


### [198] [ROMS-IMLE: A Minimalist Approach to Competitive Single-Step Generative Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19332)
*Chirag Vashist,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑了扩散模型/流匹配中"逐步将噪声变换为数据"的必要性，提出了一种极简的单步生成模型。该模型仅使用隐式最大似然估计（IMLE）作为训练目标，搭配中等规模的卷积网络，无需迭代去噪过程，在ImageNet 256上实现了2.56的FID，兼具良好的精度和召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究扩散模型/流匹配中"通过多个小变换逐步将噪声分布变换为数据分布"这一普遍信念是否真正必要，试图以极简主义方法设计具有竞争力的生成模型。

Method: 采用极简设计：训练目标选择隐式最大似然估计（IMLE），避免变分推理、对抗训练和数值积分等复杂方案；模型选择中等规模卷积网络而非Transformer；有选择地添加真正必要的元素，但排除迭代去噪过程。

Result: 单步高效生成模型在ImageNet 256上取得了2.56的FID，同时保持了良好的精度和召回率。

Conclusion: 迭代去噪并非生成模型取得优异性能的必要条件，通过极简的单步设计同样可以达到具有竞争力的生成效果。

Abstract: Generative models have undergone many generations of evolution, from VAEs/GANs to diffusion/flow matching. Along the way, the underlying techniques have become more complicated and various beliefs about what drives strong empirical performance have taken hold. Due to the success of diffusion models and flow matching, one of the more common beliefs is the importance of transforming the noise distribution to the data distribution gradually through many small transformations. We ask whether this is truly necessary, and take a minimalist approach to designing a competitive generative model. We start with the bare-bones essentials, namely just a training objective and a model. We purposefully make both simple. For the training objective, we choose Implicit Maximum Likelihood Estimation (IMLE), and eschew more complicated alternatives such as variational inference, adversarial training and numerical integration. For the model, we eschew transformers and instead choose a moderately sized convolutional network. Then we judiciously added elements that are truly essential, which surprisingly do not include iterative denoising. The result is a single-step parameter-efficient generative model that produces high quality samples at fast speed: it achieves an FID of 2.56 on ImageNet 256 and simultaneously attains good precision and recall.

</details>


### [199] [Provable diffusion-based posterior sampling for linear inverse problems via DDIM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19333)
*Yuchen Jiao,Na Li,Changxiao Cai,Yuxin Chen,Gen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PDDIM算法，通过沿测量算子的奇异方向进行坐标级DDIM更新，在观测信噪比低于扩散信噪比时遵循扩散先验，否则切换至基于测量的预测器，实现了线性逆问题的高效后验采样，具有理论收敛保证，在多个图像恢复任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的逆问题后验采样方法要么缺乏严格的理论保证，要么计算开销大，亟需一种既高效又有理论保障的算法。

Method: 提出PDDIM方法，对标准DDIM更新进行轻量级坐标级修改：沿测量算子的每个奇异方向，比较观测信噪比与扩散信噪比，低于时保留扩散先验采样，高于时切换至基于测量的校准预测器，实现后验采样。

Result: 证明了所提采样器收敛于测量条件下的贝叶斯后验；在多种图像恢复任务中，PDDIM在大多数评估指标上优于现有扩散后验采样方法。

Conclusion: 将含噪线性逆问题的后验采样转化为简单的坐标级DDIM更新，得到高效、易实现且具有后验一致性保证的算法。

Abstract: Diffusion-based methods have achieved remarkable empirical success in solving inverse problems. However, many existing posterior samplers either lack rigorous theoretical guarantees or incur substantial computational overhead. We propose a simple and efficient algorithm, called \pddim, for solving linear inverse problems with diffusion priors via a DDIM-type sampler. Our method requires only lightweight, coordinate-wise modifications to the standard DDIM update, while explicitly incorporating the measurement model. The key idea is to perform posterior sampling separately along each singular direction of the measurement operator: for each direction, the sampler follows the learned diffusion prior when the observation signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) is below the corresponding diffusion SNR, and switches to a calibrated measurement-based predictor otherwise. We prove that the proposed sampler converges to the Bayesian posterior conditioned on the measurements. Empirical results show that the proposed sampler performs favorably against existing diffusion-based posterior samplers across a range of image restoration tasks, achieving the best performance on the majority of evaluation metrics considered. Overall, our results convert posterior sampling for noisy linear inverse problems to simple coordinate-wise DDIM updates, yielding an efficient, easy-to-implement algorithm with provable posterior consistency.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [Isochoric thermodynamic preconditioning for resolving mechanically induced phase change in fractured porous media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18608)
*Veljko Lipovac,Eirik Keilegavlen,Inga Berre*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对裂隙多孔介质输运的持久变量框架，引入比体积作为独立变量，并设计了非线性预条件器来处理快速裂隙开度变化引发的瞬态相变。预条件器通过等容平衡计算初始化流体状态，能够捕捉到无预条件模拟遗漏的瞬态汽化现象。研究发现裂隙开度越大，气体含量、冷却幅度和瞬态持续时间单调增加；压力-焓与体积-温度两种形式恢复相同物理解，但压力-焓形式在再压缩为主的工况下数值鲁棒性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孔隙体积快速变化可在短于特征输运时间的时间尺度上触发相变，传统非线性求解器和自适应时间步进可能跳过这些瞬态过程，需要开发能够准确捕捉快速膨胀诱导相变的数值框架。

Method: 从全耦合系统出发，推导基于体积的模型，通过消除局部热力学变量恢复经典压力公式。引入非线性预条件器，假设瞬时自由膨胀，在推进输运问题前通过等容平衡计算求解流体状态。该预条件器适用于体积和压力两种模型。

Result: 无预条件模拟遗漏瞬态汽化，预条件模型能捕捉到。裂隙开度增大导致气体含量、膨胀冷却和瞬态持续时间单调增加。热效应影响相演化但对总持续时间影响小。裂隙开度产生显著瞬态压力降低，表明全耦合中地质力学反馈重要。压力-焓形式在再压缩工况下比体积-温度形式更鲁棒。

Conclusion: 平衡规格控制非线性问题的数值性质而非物理响应，等容预条件将非线性初始化直接与底层热力学联系起来。

Abstract: Rapid pore-volume changes can trigger phase change on timescales shorter than characteristic transport times and may therefore be skipped by conventional nonlinear solves and adaptive time stepping. We present a persistent-variable framework for transport in fractured porous media, introducing specific volume as an independent variable. Starting from a fully coupled system, we derive volume-based models and recover classical pressure-based formulations by eliminating local thermodynamic variables. To resolve abrupt fracture opening, we introduce a nonlinear preconditioner that assumes instantaneous free expansion and resolves the fluid state through an isochoric equilibrium calculation before advancing the transport problem. The preconditioner applies to both volume- and pressure-based models. In the studied fracture-opening cases, unpreconditioned simulations miss transient vaporization, whereas the preconditioned models do not. Across the investigated aperture range, larger openings produce monotonic increases in gas content, expansion-induced cooling, and durations of transients. Thermal effects alter the phase evolution but have otherwise minor influence on the overall transient duration. Within the proof-of-concept setting, fracture opening generates substantial transient pressure reductions, indicating that geomechanical feedback may become important in fully coupled applications. Pressure-enthalpy and volume-temperature formulations recover identical physical solutions but exhibit different nonlinear robustness, with the pressure-enthalpy formulation proving more robust in some recompression-dominated cases. These results show that equilibrium specifications control the numerical properties of the nonlinear problem rather than recovered physical responses, while isochoric preconditioning connects the nonlinear initialization directly to the underlying thermodynamics.

</details>


### [201] [Uncertainty quantification in mechanics: A unified Bayesian perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18734)
*Sascha Ranftl,Malte Rolf,Gerhard A. Holzapfel,Ellen Kuhl*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统阐述了贝叶斯概率论作为不确定量化的统一理论框架，涵盖前向和逆向两类问题（参数校准与代理模型构建），并特别强调了该框架在生物力学中的应用，包括模型选择和实验设计等子问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算力学中不确定量化面临两大问题——前向问题（输入不确定性如何传播到输出）和逆向问题（从实验或模拟中推断未知参数）。高效传播通常需要大量计算，因此需要快速的数据驱动代理模型。作者旨在提供一个统一的理论框架来解决这些挑战。

Method: 基于贝叶斯概率论和部分信念（partial belief）概念，构建统一的理论框架。该框架可同时处理两类逆向任务：（i）输入不确定性的识别与校准，（ii）代理模型的构建。框架还无缝整合了模型选择（识别最可能的模型规范）和实验设计（优化数据收集以最大化关于参数的信息增益）等子问题。

Result: 论证了贝叶斯概率论能够统一处理前向和逆向不确定量化问题，并且能够自然融入模型选择、实验设计、敏感性分析及随机场等特殊先验的使用。特别在生物力学中，该框架能有效应对生物异质性、患者特异性变异和噪声数据带来的显著不确定性。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯方法为计算力学中的不确定量化提供了统一的理论框架，在生物力学等变异性和不确定性显著的领域具有特殊优势。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential to experimental mechanics, but has become particularly relevant in computational mechanics, manifesting in two fundamental problem types: forward and inverse problems. The former addresses how input uncertainties propagate to the quantities of interest, whereas the latter aims to infer unknown parameters from experimental observations or simulations. Since efficient propagation typically requires a prohibitive number of evaluations to compute marginal output distributions, the development of fast, data-driven surrogate models becomes necessary. Thus, we can distinguish between two inverse tasks: (i) the identification and calibration of input uncertainties, and (ii) the construction of surrogates, a methodology collectively referred to as surrogate-based UQ. Building on probabilistic reasoning and the concept of partial belief, we demonstrate that Bayesian probability theory provides a unified theoretical framework for addressing both problem types. We further show that Bayesian inference allows for the seamless incorporation of essential subproblems, including model selection for identifying the most probable model specifications and experimental design for optimizing data collection by identifying experiments or simulations that maximize expected information gain about parameters, among others such as connections to sensitivity analysis or the use of special priors like random fields. While this theoretical framework is presented for general mechanical problems, particular emphasis is placed on biomechanics, where variability and uncertainty is especially pronounced due to inherent biological heterogeneity, patient-specific variability, and noisy data.

</details>


### [202] [Random site percolation with complex neighborhoods in five dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18991)
*Krzysztof Malarz,Maciej Wołoszyn*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了五维空间中复杂邻域的随机位点渗流问题，通过改进的Newman-Ziff算法实现了巨大加速（等效减少2170年单核计算时间），首次计算了120个渗流阈值，并验证了渗流阈值与加权配位数之间的普适幂律关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究五维空间中复杂邻域的渗流问题，特别是针对由多个配位区（最高至第七配位区）构成的邻域，计算其渗流阈值和分形维数，并验证渗流阈值与加权配位数之间的普适标度关系。

Method: 采用经典Newman-Ziff算法的改进C++实现（含普通工作划分），通过并行化显著加速计算；对127种复杂邻域（含7种紧致邻域）进行渗流阈值计算，并利用标度关系验证分形维数。

Result: 计算了127个渗流阈值（其中120个为首次估计）；7种紧致邻域的分形维数均值为3.5581(70)，与理论预测值的误差在0.26%-1.76%之间；验证了渗流阈值与加权配位数ζ的幂律关系：p_c ∝ ζ^{-g}，g≈0.7913(43)。

Conclusion: 五维空间中复杂邻域的渗流行为遵循与加权配位数相关的普适幂律关系，改进的Newman-Ziff算法能高效处理高维复杂邻域渗流问题，所获分形维数与标度理论预测高度一致。

Abstract: In this paper, the random site percolation problem in a five-dimensional space for complex neighborhoods is studied. The efficient C++ code (with ordinary work division) of the classical Newman--Ziff algorithm is presented. The obtained speed-up of computations reduces 2170 years of single-core computations -- necessary for obtaining the results presented in this paper -- much below the typical half-decay time of the scientist. For complex neighborhoods, it is for neighborhoods composed with sites taken from several coordination zones (up to the seventh coordination zone), the 127 percolation thresholds are calculated (with 120 among them being estimated for the first time). For seven extended (compact) neighborhoods, the fractal dimensions are also calculated. The mean value of these fractal dimensions, averaged over these seven compact neighborhoods, is estimated as $\langle d_f\rangle\approx 3.5581(70)$. The percentage errors of the values obtained for the fractal dimensions for compact neighborhoods vary from 0.26\% to 1.76\% with respect to the theoretically predicted value based on scaling relations and the most recent estimates of critical exponents for five-dimensional space. The universality of the percolation threshold as dependent on the weighted coordination number $ζ=\sum_i z_i r_i$ (where $z_i$ is the number of sites in the $i$-th coordination zone and $r_i$ is the Euclidean distance from the sites in the $i$-th coordination zone to the central site) is also verified. The latter manifests itself as the power law ($p_c\proptoζ^{-g}$) with $g\approx 0.7913(43)$

</details>


### [203] [An interaction potential method for passive and active dynamics of hyperelastic materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19227)
*Francesco De Vita,Filippo Caruso Lombardi,Roberto Verzicco,Francesco Viola*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于四面体边应变的相互作用势公式，用于模拟主动生物组织（如心肌），该方法桥接了连续介质超弹性力学与离散相互作用势求解器，通过边应变表示常应变四面体单元，保持边缘间的能量耦合，并引入主动应变乘法分解和体积不可压缩约束策略，数值实验验证了其在不同超弹性本构下的有效性及在真实心室主动变形模拟中的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限元方法在模拟主动生物组织时需要全局非线性求解，计算成本高；而快速相互作用势方法将连续体近似为独立链接网络，牺牲了物理保真度。现有方法在这两种范式之间存在差距，缺乏既能保持连续体力学的物理准确性又具备高效计算特性的方法。

Method: 将连续介质超弹性本构律重新表述为四面体边应变形式；通过主动应变乘法分解引入主动收缩，实现为时间依赖的激活参考构型；提出避免体积锁定的不可压缩约束策略；将整体方法解释为常应变四面体连续单元的边应变表示。

Result: 数值测试表明该方法能有效模拟不同超弹性本构律，正确保持能量平衡方程；在真实心室主动变形模拟中，复现了与文献一致的纵向缩短和壁厚增厚值。

Conclusion: 该方法成功桥接了连续介质力学与离散相互作用势求解器，兼具物理保真度与计算效率，为主动生物组织模拟提供了一种新的有效途径。

Abstract: Simulating active biological tissues, such as the myocardium, requires constitutive models that are both physically faithful and computationally efficient. The most common approach relies on finite element methods that accurately discretize the underlying continuum hyperelastic problem which, in turn, require global nonlinear solve at each time step. On the other hand, fast, interaction-potential methods replace the continuum with a network of independent links approximating the mechanical response. We propose an interaction potential formulation for simulating active biological tissues that bridges this gap. The method recasts continuum hyperelastic constitutive laws in terms of tetrahedral edge strain. Unlike classical mass-spring models, the proposed formulation does not approximate the tissue as independent spring elements but preserves the energetic coupling between adjacent edges. Passive tissue mechanics is described by hyperelastic constitutive laws, while active contraction is incorporated through the active-strain multiplicative decomposition. Within the edge-based formulation, the active strain is incorporated through a time-dependent activated reference configuration. We further introduce a strategy for enforcing the material incompressibility constraint while avoiding volumetric locking. The resulting method can be interpreted as an edge-strain representation of a constant-strain tetrahedral continuum element, providing a bridge between continuum mechanics and discrete interaction potential solvers. Numerical tests demonstrate the capability of the method to effectively simulate different hyperelastic constitutive laws and to properly preserve the energy balance equation. Finally, the proposed method is applied to the active deformation of a realistic ventricle, reproducing longitudinal shortening and wall-thickening values consistent with the literature.

</details>
